- **qraspi-architecture**: Start the QRASPI Architecture phase -- lock the path-dependent decisions for a new system as MADR ADRs with alternatives, draw the C4 Context + Container in Mermaid, and specify the required fitness functions. Use for "/qraspi-architecture &lt;project&gt;". Reads research.md and writes docs/adr/NNNN-*.md + architecture.md. ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/qraspi-architecture.md) · ★3)
- **qraspi-graduate**: Graduate a NEW system from QRASPI (greenfield) to QRSPI (brownfield) -- once the walking skeleton is green and V0/V1 is shipped, capture the repo + ADRs + skeleton state + fitness functions + stack into graduation.md and hand new feature work to QRSPI. Use for "/qraspi-graduate &lt;project&gt;". Terminal -- do not run mid-workflow. Reads skeleton.md + implementation logs, writes graduation.md. ([michaelalber/ai-toolkit](https://github.com/michaelalber/ai-toolkit/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/qraspi-graduate.md) · ★3)
- **qraspi-graduate**: Graduate a NEW system from QRASPI (greenfield) to QRSPI (brownfield) -- once the walking skeleton is green and V0/V1 is shipped, capture the repo + ADRs + skeleton state + fitness functions + stack into graduation.md and hand new feature work to QRSPI.
